- **engagement-manager**: Use this agent as the quality gate and synthesis layer for consulting engagements. Invoke it (typically from the /engagement command) to review specialist workstream outputs against partner-level quality standards, resolve cross-workstream inconsistencies, and synthesize the answer-first storyline. - **engine-concurrency-reviewer**: Enforces stugan's engine-loop concurrency discipline on changed code — state is mutated only on the engine loop goroutine, read concurrently under e.mu (RWMutex), and Snapshot()/SnapshotNetwork() must deep-copy so a live pointer into engine state never escapes to a reader. Use after touching internal/core/engine.go, network.go, types.go, command.go, or anything that reads/mutates the User→Network→Channel tree. ([klppl/stugan](https://github.com/klppl/stugan/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/engine-concurrency-reviewer.md) · ★2)
